# Timeline - what happened, when, and how long it took

Jaron, 2026-09-26: "a file that logs the day things are done and the time things are done, even when they're discussed - it keeps
track of how long things actually take, and when conversations compact you can see what was happening and what was achieved."

Rules: one line per event, Fort Wayne time, newest at the bottom. **idea** = discussed/asked, **done** = shipped and verified,
**fix** = repair. Add a line the moment something is decided or shipped. Durations are idea -> done.

## 2026-09-24 (Thu)
- ~4:40 PM idea: Heartbeat News (Vex + Joe, own tab, 3D newsroom) - done ~8:00 PM by a cloud session (~3 h 20 m).
- ~8 PM done: Voice Studio saves for everyone.
- ~10:14 PM idea: update the share card - done 10:21 PM (7 m). ~10:30 PM done: Vercel Analytics on every page.
- 10:24 PM: first X boost ($25) - paused by X ~10:34 PM ("Financial products" - trading line). Post deleted.

- 2026-09-27 10:36 - Jalen gray skin ROOT CAUSE found in log: skin shader fails to compile on SM5 ("maximum ps_5_0 sampler register index (16) exceeded") so Unreal draws its default gray material. Fix: project switched to SM6 (Config/DefaultEngine.ini WindowsTargetSettings D3D12TargetedShaderFormats=PCD3D_SM6). Re-render running. Jaron used his free usage reset (0% week).
- 2026-09-27 10:47 - Jalen renders in COLOR. Real cause: the face skin bake runs through M_skin_unified_UI, which failed to compile on SM5 (16-sampler limit), so T_Head_BC baked pure gray (chroma 0). Fix: project -> SM6, rebuilt Cinematic to /Game/MetaHumansSM6/Jalen (portrait scripts now default there). Picture: heartbeatobservatory.com/tv/drafts/jalen-color-1.jpg. ~2.5h total on the gray bug.
